(function(){
var toast=document.getElementById('toast'),tt;
function say(m){toast.textContent=m;toast.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(tt);tt=setTimeout(function(){toast.classList.remove('on')},1600)}
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  var c=e.target.closest('[data-copy]'); if(c){var el=document.getElementById(c.getAttribute('data-copy')),txt=el.textContent;
    (navigator.clipboard?navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt):Promise.reject()).then(function(){say('Copied')}).catch(function(){var r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(el);var s=getSelection();s.removeAllRanges();s.addRange(r);say('Selected — press Copy')});}
  var l=e.target.closest('[data-lang]'); if(l){var v=l.getAttribute('data-lang');document.querySelectorAll('[data-lang]').forEach(function(b){b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b===l))});document.querySelectorAll('[data-pane]').forEach(function(p){p.hidden=p.getAttribute('data-pane')!==v});}
  var z=e.target.closest('[data-zip]'); if(z){zip(z);}
});
function zip(btn){
  if(!window.JSZip){say('ZIP tool is still loading — try again');return;}
  var sel=btn.getAttribute('data-zip'),files=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(sel==='all'?'[data-file]':'#'+sel+' [data-file]'));
  var z=new JSZip(),done=0,label=btn.textContent; btn.disabled=true;
  Promise.all(files.map(function(a){return fetch(a.href).then(function(r){return r.blob()}).then(function(b){z.file(a.getAttribute('data-file'),b);done++;btn.textContent='Preparing '+done+'/'+files.length+'…';})}))
  .then(function(){return z.generateAsync({type:'blob'})}).then(function(b){var u=URL.createObjectURL(b),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=(sel==='all'?'baby-mo-app-store-kit':sel)+'.zip';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(u)},4000);say('Download started');})
  .catch(function(){say('Download failed — check your connection')}).then(function(){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=label;});
}

  cb.addEventListener('change',function(){try{localStorage.setItem(k,cb.checked?'1':'0')}catch(e){}cb.closest('li').classList.toggle('done',cb.checked)})});

fe:Tdf0,Baby Mo adalah teman belajar Islam untuk anak usia 2–8 tahun. Si kecil belajar doa, sholat, wudhu, dan huruf hijaiyah lewat 19 game yang seru — bersama Baby Mo, sahabat kecilnya.

Tanpa iklan. Tanpa akun. Bisa dimainkan tanpa internet.
Sekali beli, semua game terbuka — tanpa langganan dan tanpa pembelian dalam aplikasi.

BARU DI VERSI 1.3
• Waktu Main & Sholat untuk orang tua: jeda istirahat, batas main 60 atau 120 menit sehari, dan pengingat “Waktunya sholat” di setiap waktu sholat — semuanya dijaga pertanyaan untuk orang dewasa.
• Mewarnai Baby Mo: 40 gambar dengan krayon ajaib, glitter, dan pelangi.
• Kata Baik: 40 kata baik, masing-masing dengan alat musiknya sendiri — 24 alat musik, dari angklung dan sasando sampai piano.
• Memory Match lebih mudah untuk balita, 5 ruangan baru di Cari Baby Mo, dan suasana tidur baru di Waktunya Tidur.

SEMUA YANG ADA DI BABY MO
• Doa Harian — 14 doa dari bangun tidur sampai tidur lagi, dengan suara Arab, latin, dan arti.
• Sholat Bareng Mo — panduan gerakan dan bacaan sholat, jadwal sholat hari ini, dan arah kiblat.
• Wudhu Bareng Mo — 10 langkah wudhu lengkap dengan niat dan doa, plus game mengurutkan langkah.
• Huruf Hijaiyah — 28 huruf Arab dalam 9 pelajaran bertahap, dengan suara, menulis, kuis, dan bintang.
• Pulau Hijaiyah — jelajahi pulau kotak-kotak bersama Baby Mo dan temukan 28 huruf yang tersembunyi.
• Kata Islam (Susun Huruf) — 106 kata Arab dalam 12 kategori, disusun huruf demi huruf.
• Akademi Tenis Kak Adam — 6 latihan dari bola merah sampai pertandingan pertama: tunggu pantulan, backhand dua tangan, forehand dan backhand, langkah kaki, servis, dan skor love-15-30-40.
• Sahabat Hewan Mo — merawat hewan peternakan dengan adab dan kasih sayang.
• Kebun Kecil Ais — berkebun dari benih sampai panen.
• Dua Journey — cocokkan doa dengan momennya.
• Memory Match — 7 level, dari 4 kartu untuk balita sampai 20 kartu untuk juara.
• Cari Baby Mo — temukan Baby Mo yang bersembunyi di kamar, ruang keluarga, sampai pasar Ramadan.
• Kenalan dengan Baby Mo — puzzle interaktif untuk perkenalan, plus 40 gambar untuk mewarnai Mo.
• Kata Baik — 40 kalimat thayyibah dan kata-kata yang baik, masing-masing dengan alat musiknya sendiri.
• Forehand Beat — game ritme yang seru.
• Dreamtime — rutinitas tidur 4 langkah: gosok gigi, ganti baju, baca doa, lalu tidur.
• Doa Ramadan — 11 doa Ramadan dengan suara: hilal, niat puasa, berbuka, sampai takbir.
• Tasbih Sebelum Tidur — Subhanallah 33, Alhamdulillah 33, Allahu akbar 34, manik demi manik.
• Streak Sholat Mo — jadwal sholat, arah kiblat, dan streak harian untuk membiasakan sholat.

DIRANCANG UNTUK SI KECIL
• Tombol besar yang mudah ditekan jari mungil
• Suara lebih dulu, jadi anak yang belum bisa membaca tetap bisa bermain
• Bintang hadiah di setiap game
• Baby Mo menyapa si kecil dengan namanya
• Mode terang dan gelap
• Bahasa Indonesia dan Inggris

AMAN UNTUK ANAK
• Tanpa iklan, tanpa langganan, dan tanpa pembelian dalam aplikasi
• Tanpa akun, tanpa chat, tanpa fitur sosial
• Orang tua bisa mengatur jeda istirahat, batas main harian, dan pengingat sholat
• Tidak mengumpulkan data pribadi anak — nama dan progres hanya tersimpan di perangkat
• Lokasi bersifat opsional, hanya untuk jadwal sholat dan kiblat; bisa memilih kota sendiri

Doa, ayat, dan hadits disertai sumbernya. Ada pertanyaan atau saran? Hubungi kami di hello@babymo.id.

101:Td62,Baby Mo is an Islamic learning companion for children aged 2–8. Little ones learn du'as, prayer, wudu and the hijaiyah letters through 19 fun games — together with Baby Mo, their little friend.

No ads. No account. Works offline.
One purchase unlocks every game — no subscriptions and no in-app purchases.

NEW IN VERSION 1.3
• Play time & prayer for parents: break reminders, a 60 or 120-minute daily limit and a “Time to pray” pop-up at each prayer time — all behind a grown-up question.
• Colour Baby Mo: 40 colouring pages with a magic crayon, glitter and rainbows.
• Good Words: 40 good words, each with its own instrument — 24 in all, from angklung and sasando to piano.
• Memory Match made easier for toddlers, 5 new Find Baby Mo rooms and new bedtime scenes in Dreamtime.

EVERYTHING INSIDE
• Daily Du'as — 14 du'as from waking up to going back to sleep, with Arabic audio, transliteration and meaning.
• Pray with Mo — a guide to the prayer movements and recitations, today's prayer times and the Qibla direction.
• Wudu with Mo — the 10 steps of wudu with the intention and du'a, plus a sorting game.
• Hijaiyah Letters — the 28 Arabic letters in 9 step-by-step lessons, with sound, tracing, quizzes and stars.
• Hijaiyah Island — explore a blocky island with Baby Mo and find all 28 hidden letters.
• Islamic Words (Build the Word) — 106 Arabic words in 12 categories, built letter by letter.
• Kak Adam's Tennis Academy — 6 drills from the red ball to a first match: wait for the bounce, two-handed backhand, forehand and backhand, footwork, serving and love-15-30-40 scoring.
• Mo's Animal Friends — caring for farm animals with good manners and kindness.
• Ais's Little Garden — gardening from seed to harvest.
• Dua Journey — match each du'a to its moment.
• Memory Match — 7 levels, from 4 cards for toddlers to 20 cards for champions.
• Find Baby Mo — find Baby Mo hiding in his bedroom, the living room and even a Ramadan market.
• Meet Baby Mo — an interactive getting-to-know-you puzzle, plus 40 pages for colouring Mo.
• Good Words — 40 good, kind words (kalimat thayyibah), each with its own instrument.
• Forehand Beat — a fun rhythm game.
• Dreamtime — a 4-step bedtime routine: brush teeth, change clothes, say the du'a, then sleep.
• Ramadan Du'as — 11 Ramadan du'as with audio: the crescent, the fast, iftar and the takbir.
• Bedtime Tasbih — Subhanallah 33, Alhamdulillah 33, Allahu akbar 34, bead by bead.
• Prayer Streak with Mo — prayer times, the Qibla direction and a daily streak to build the habit.

DESIGNED FOR LITTLE ONES
• Big buttons for small fingers
• Sound first, so children who can't read yet can still play
• Reward stars in every game
• Baby Mo greets your child by name
• Light and dark mode
• Indonesian and English

SAFE FOR CHILDREN
• No ads, no subscriptions and no in-app purchases
• No accounts, no chat, no social features
• Parents can set play breaks, a daily play limit and prayer reminders
• No personal data collected from children — names and progress stay on the device
• Location is optional and only used for prayer times and the Qibla; you can pick a city instead

Du'as, verses and hadith come with their sources. Questions or ideas? Write to us at hello@babymo.id.

Privacy: https://babymo.id/privacy/

104:T567,Baru di versi 1.3
• BARU — Waktu Main & Sholat untuk orang tua: pilih jeda istirahat setiap 10, 20, atau 30 menit dan batas main 60 atau 120 menit sehari. Setiap masuk waktu sholat, Mo muncul dengan “Waktunya sholat” dan menunggu sampai si kecil selesai sholat. Ada di tab Orang Tua — setiap perubahan diawali pertanyaan untuk orang dewasa.
• Kenalan dengan Baby Mo: kini bisa mewarnai Baby Mo! 40 gambar dengan krayon ajaib yang memberi warna asli Mo, krayon glitter dan pelangi, serta bintang yang bangun saat diwarnai.
• Kata Baik: kini 40 kata baik, masing-masing dengan alat musiknya sendiri — 24 alat musik, dari angklung, gamelan, dan sasando sampai piano dan gitar. Selesaikan satu kata, dengarkan lagu pendek Baby Mo, lalu Mo membacakan kalimat Arab dan artinya.
• Memory Match: lebih mudah untuk balita, satu tombol Main yang besar, setiap kartu menyebut namanya, gambar baru untuk tema buah, hewan, dan Ramadan, serta kartu huruf hijaiyah.
• Cari Baby Mo: 5 ruangan petak umpet baru.
• Forehand Beat: Mo berlari, melompat, dan memukul, dengan suara yang menyemangati setiap pukulan.
• Waktunya Tidur: suasana baru — gosok gigi, piyama, doa, dan tempat tidur yang nyaman — dengan setiap kata doa menyala saat Mo membacanya.
• Ikon aplikasi baru.
• Layar lebih tenang (lebih sedikit gelembung bicara) dan suara lebih andal di iPhone dan iPad.105:T4e7,New in version 1.3
• NEW — Play time & prayer, for parents: choose a break every 10, 20 or 30 minutes and a daily limit of 60 or 120 minutes. At each prayer time Mo pops up with “Time to pray” and waits until your child has prayed. Find it in the Parents tab — every change asks a grown-up question first.
• Meet Baby Mo: now you can colour Baby Mo! 40 colouring pages with a magic crayon that paints Mo's real colours, glitter and rainbow crayons, and stars that wake up as you colour.
• Good Words: now 40 good words, each with its own instrument — 24 in all, from angklung, gamelan and sasando to piano and guitar. Finish a word, hear a short Baby Mo song, then Mo reads the Arabic and its meaning.
• Memory Match: easier for toddlers, one big Play button, every card says its name, new drawn cards for fruit, animals and Ramadan, and Hijaiyah letter cards.
• Find Baby Mo: 5 new hide-and-seek rooms.
• Forehand Beat: Mo runs, jumps and swings, with a voice that cheers every hit.
• Dreamtime: new bedtime scenes — brushing teeth, pyjamas, du'a and a cosy bed — with each du'a word lighting up as Mo reads it.
• A fresh app icon.

• Waktu sholat untuk seluruh dunia: cari kota atau negara mana pun — lebih dari 27.000 tempat — tanpa internet. Metode hitungannya otomatis mengikuti negaranya (Kemenag di Indonesia, JAKIM, MUIS, Umm al-Qura, dan lainnya) dan bisa diganti di “Metode”, termasuk Ashar Hanafi.

• Apple Watch — Arah Kiblat: Mo menunjukkan arah kiblat dengan kompas yang bersinar emas saat jam sudah menghadap Ka’bah.

• Prayer times worldwide: search any city or country — more than 27,000 places — with no internet needed. The calculation method follows the country automatically (Kemenag in Indonesia, JAKIM, MUIS, Umm al-Qura and more) and can be changed under “Method”, including Hanafi Asr.
• A city in another time zone shows its own local times, with the countdown to the next prayer.
• Apple Watch — Qibla: Mo shows the way to the Qibla on a compass that glows gold when the watch faces the Kaaba.
• Apple Watch — Wake up with Mo: a morning alarm on the days you choose; tap it and Mo reads the waking-up du'a.
• Apple Watch — Ramadan practice fast: half a day or until Maghrib, with a lantern lit for every day of practice.
• Two new watch-face complications: “Du'a now” and the Ramadan lanterns.
• Meet Baby Mo: a new end screen — get to know Mo, what he loves, and his family and friends.
• In the Parents tab, tap Version to check for updates.

• BARU — Baby Mo di Apple Watch: doa harian dengan suara Mo, tasbih (putar Digital Crown atau goyangkan tangan), waktu sholat dengan pengingat wudhu, hitung mundur Ramadan, dan merawat Mimi si kucing — plus komplikasi “sholat berikutnya” untuk tampilan jam.
• Doa Baby Mo sebagai aksi Shortcuts: pasang di otomasi “saat CarPlay terhubung”, dan Mo membacakan doa naik kendaraan lewat speaker mobil.
• Tema Ramadan: setiap bulan Ramadan, Baby Mo berganti tampilan — langit senja, lentera, kartu “Ramadan hari ke-…” dengan hitungan hari menuju Idul Fitri, dan sapaan “Marhaban ya Ramadan”. Saat Idul Fitri ada hiasan ketupat dan ucapan lebaran.
• Susun Huruf kini punya 106 kata Arab dalam 12 kategori (keluarga, hewan, buah, pakaian, hewan laut, dan lainnya), tingkat Mudah, Sedang, dan Sulit, ronde 5 kata, dan Album Stikerku.

• Waktunya Tidur: langkah malam di bawah langit berbintang dan tombol bilas berbentuk tetes air.
• Doa Harian: tombol Baca Bersama Mo yang selalu terlihat, perayaan baru setelah membaca doa, dan Tasbih Sebelum Tidur dengan pilihan Baca atau Tasbih.
• Layar menang yang lebih rapi, dengan Baby Mo mengintip dari balik kartu.
• Tampilan yang pas di iPad (termasuk Split View dan Stage Manager) dan di iPhone Duo, terlipat maupun terbuka.
• Perbaikan kecil: ulat di kebun lebih mudah dipindah, satu kalimat tidak lagi dibaca dua kali, dan perbaikan tampilan lainnya.109:T5d9,New in version 1.1

• Baby Mo Du'a as a Shortcuts action: add it to a “When CarPlay connects” automation and Mo recites the travel du'a through your car speakers.

• Build the Word now has 106 Arabic words in 12 categories (family, animals, fruit, clothes, sea animals and more), Easy, Medium and Hard levels, 5-word rounds and a sticker album.

• Tidier win screens, with Baby Mo peeking out from behind the card.
• A better fit on iPad (including Split View and Stage Manager) and on iPhone Duo, folded or unfolded.

3f:["$","h2",null,{"id":"review","children":"Notes for App Review"}]
10e:Tb4b,Baby Mo is a paid Islamic learning app (one-time purchase) for children aged 2–8 with 19 games: daily and Ramadan du'as, prayer and wudhu guides, Arabic letters and 106 Arabic words, a letter-hunt island, a tennis-learning game, caring for farm animals and a little garden. There are no accounts, ads, subscriptions, in-app purchases, chat or social features.

• No login is needed: open the app and choose a language.
• Location is optional and only used for prayer times and the Qibla compass in the Prayer tab. If permission is denied, the user can search for a city by hand — the list of 27,000+ places (GeoNames, credited in the app) is bundled, so the search works offline. Prayer times and the Qibla are calculated on the device; no location or other data ever leaves the device.
• The child's nickname, stars and progress are stored only on the device.
• Arabic read-aloud plays recordings bundled with the app (the device's speech voice is a fallback). There are no analytics or third-party SDKs, and every link out of the app and every share option is behind a parental gate (a maths question for grown-ups).
• All games work offline. The app's only network request is the update check on the Version row in the Parents tab: at most once a day it asks Apple's App Store lookup service (itunes.apple.com/lookup, with the app's bundle ID only — no user data) whether a newer version exists. Offline it simply does nothing, and opening the App Store from there is behind the parental gate.
• The archive includes a small Apple Watch app: five daily du'as in the same recorded voice, a tasbih counter, on-device prayer times (the parent picks a city by hand — the watch never uses location), a Qibla compass for that city (compass heading only, no location permission), Ramadan countdowns with a practice-fast tracker, and a simple pet check-in. Optional reminders — wudu, suhoor/iftar and the “Wake up with Mo” morning alarm — are local notifications; the watch asks for notification permission only when a parent turns one on. Like the iPhone app it is fully offline, has no accounts and collects no data.
• “Baby Mo Du'a” is also a Shortcuts action: it plays the travel du'a from the bundled recordings, and a parent can add it to a CarPlay automation. It sends nothing off the device.
• During Ramadan (Umm al-Qura calendar on the device) the home screen shows a Ramadan look; it switches on and off by itself.
• Parents tab → Play Time & Prayer (new in 1.3): an optional break timer (every 10/20/30 minutes), a daily play limit (60/120 minutes) and a “Time to pray” reminder at each prayer time. Everything is worked out on the device, and every change, or any extra time, asks the parental gate first. To see the reminder, set a city in the Prayer tab.

112:T567,Baru di versi 1.3
• BARU — Waktu Main & Sholat untuk orang tua: pilih jeda istirahat setiap 10, 20, atau 30 menit dan batas main 60 atau 120 menit sehari. Setiap masuk waktu sholat, Mo muncul dengan “Waktunya sholat” dan menunggu sampai si kecil selesai sholat. Ada di tab Orang Tua — setiap perubahan diawali pertanyaan untuk orang dewasa.
• Kenalan dengan Baby Mo: kini bisa mewarnai Baby Mo! 40 gambar dengan krayon ajaib yang memberi warna asli Mo, krayon glitter dan pelangi, serta bintang yang bangun saat diwarnai.
• Kata Baik: kini 40 kata baik, masing-masing dengan alat musiknya sendiri — 24 alat musik, dari angklung, gamelan, dan sasando sampai piano dan gitar. Selesaikan satu kata, dengarkan lagu pendek Baby Mo, lalu Mo membacakan kalimat Arab dan artinya.
• Memory Match: lebih mudah untuk balita, satu tombol Main yang besar, setiap kartu menyebut namanya, gambar baru untuk tema buah, hewan, dan Ramadan, serta kartu huruf hijaiyah.
• Cari Baby Mo: 5 ruangan petak umpet baru.
• Forehand Beat: Mo berlari, melompat, dan memukul, dengan suara yang menyemangati setiap pukulan.
• Waktunya Tidur: suasana baru — gosok gigi, piyama, doa, dan tempat tidur yang nyaman — dengan setiap kata doa menyala saat Mo membacanya.
• Ikon aplikasi baru.
• Layar lebih tenang (lebih sedikit gelembung bicara) dan suara lebih andal di iPhone dan iPad.100:["$","div","f-id-5",{"className":"field","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"fhead","children":[["$","div",null,{"children":[["$","b",null,{"children":"What's New in This Version"}]," ",["$","small",null,{"children":["· ","Version → What's New (only shown for updates, not for 1.0)"]}]]}],["$","div",null,{"style":{"display":"flex","gap":10,"alignItems":"center"},"children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"count","children":[1351," / ",4000]}],["$","button",null,{"className":"btn copy","type":"button","data-copy":"f-id-5","children":"Copy"}]]}]]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"val","id":"f-id-5","children":"$112"}]]}]

113:T4e7,New in version 1.3
• NEW — Play time & prayer, for parents: choose a break every 10, 20 or 30 minutes and a daily limit of 60 or 120 minutes. At each prayer time Mo pops up with “Time to pray” and waits until your child has prayed. Find it in the Parents tab — every change asks a grown-up question first.
• Meet Baby Mo: now you can colour Baby Mo! 40 colouring pages with a magic crayon that paints Mo's real colours, glitter and rainbow crayons, and stars that wake up as you colour.
• Good Words: now 40 good words, each with its own instrument — 24 in all, from angklung, gamelan and sasando to piano and guitar. Finish a word, hear a short Baby Mo song, then Mo reads the Arabic and its meaning.
• Memory Match: easier for toddlers, one big Play button, every card says its name, new drawn cards for fruit, animals and Ramadan, and Hijaiyah letter cards.
• Find Baby Mo: 5 new hide-and-seek rooms.
• Forehand Beat: Mo runs, jumps and swings, with a voice that cheers every hit.
• Dreamtime: new bedtime scenes — brushing teeth, pyjamas, du'a and a cosy bed — with each du'a word lighting up as Mo reads it.
• A fresh app icon.
• Calmer screens (fewer pop-up speech bubbles) and more reliable sound on iPhone and iPad.103:["$","div","f-en-5",{"className":"field","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"fhead","children":[["$","div",null,{"children":[["$","b",null,{"children":"What's New in This Version"}]," ",["$","small",null,{"children":["· ","Version → What's New (only shown for updates, not for 1.0)"]}]]}],["$","div",null,{"style":{"display":"flex","gap":10,"alignItems":"center"},"children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"count","children":[1223," / ",4000]}],["$","button",null,{"className":"btn copy","type":"button","data-copy":"f-en-5","children":"Copy"}]]}]]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"val","id":"f-en-5","children":"$113"}]]}]
